*Yuchen Shi,Huajie Chen,Heng Xu,Zhiquan Liu,Jialiang Shen,Chi Liu,Shuai Zhou,Tianqing Zhu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为Osmosis Distillation (OD)攻击的新型模型劫持策略，揭示了在基于数据集蒸馏生成的合成数据集上进行迁移学习时存在的安全威胁，攻击者只需在合成数据集中加入少量毒化样本即可实现模型劫持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习和数据集蒸馏的结合在评估中表现出色，但使用数据集蒸馏方法生成的合成数据集进行迁移学习时存在尚未被发现的安全威胁。攻击者可以通过在合成数据集中添加少量毒化样本实施模型劫持攻击，这种威胁需要被揭示和认识。

Method: 提出了Osmosis Distillation (OD)攻击方法，这是一种针对深度学习模型的新型模型劫持策略，使用最少的样本实现攻击。该方法通过在合成数据集中注入毒化样本来劫持模型，使其在保持原始任务性能的同时执行隐藏任务。

Result: 在各种数据集上的综合评估表明，OD攻击在隐藏任务中实现了高攻击成功率，同时在原始任务中保持了高模型效用。蒸馏的渗透集支持跨不同模型架构的模型劫持，在迁移学习中实现了显著的攻击性能和模型效用。

Conclusion: 使用第三方合成数据集进行迁移学习存在严重的安全风险，必须提高对此类威胁的认识。OD攻击揭示了数据集蒸馏与迁移学习结合时的安全漏洞，强调了在采用合成数据集时需要加强安全防护。

Abstract: Transfer learning is devised to leverage knowledge from pre-trained models to solve new tasks with limited data and computational resources. Meanwhile, dataset distillation has emerged to synthesize a compact dataset that preserves critical information from the original large dataset. Therefore, a combination of transfer learning and dataset distillation offers promising performance in evaluations. However, a non-negligible security threat remains undiscovered in transfer learning using synthetic datasets generated by dataset distillation methods, where an adversary can perform a model hijacking attack with only a few poisoned samples in the synthetic dataset. To reveal this threat, we propose Osmosis Distillation (OD) attack, a novel model hijacking strategy that targets deep learning models using the fewest samples. Comprehensive evaluations on various datasets demonstrate that the OD attack attains high attack success rates in hidden tasks while preserving high model utility in original tasks. Furthermore, the distilled osmosis set enables model hijacking across diverse model architectures, allowing model hijacking in transfer learning with considerable attack performance and model utility. We argue that awareness of using third-party synthetic datasets in transfer learning must be raised.

</details>


### [115] [AgentSCOPE: Evaluating Contextual Privacy Across Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04902)
*Ivoline C. Ngong,Keerthiram Murugesan,Swanand Kadhe,Justin D. Weisz,Amit Dhurandhar,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Privacy Flow Graph框架，基于情境完整性理论分解智能体执行流程，识别中间信息流中的隐私泄露风险，并创建AgentSCOPE基准测试，发现超过80%场景存在隐私泄露，其中24%即使最终输出看似干净也存在中间泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统隐私评估主要关注输入和输出边界，但忽略了任务执行过程中的中间信息流（如智能体查询、工具响应等），这些中间环节同样存在隐私泄露风险，需要独立评估。

Method: 提出Privacy Flow Graph框架，基于情境完整性理论将智能体执行流程分解为一系列信息流，每个信息流用五个CI参数标注，能够追踪隐私泄露到其源头；创建AgentSCOPE基准，包含8个监管领域的62个多工具场景，每个管道阶段都有真实标注。

Result: 对7个最先进的LLM评估显示：超过80%的场景在管道中存在隐私泄露，即使最终输出看似干净的场景中也有24%存在泄露；大多数泄露发生在工具响应阶段，API会不加区分地返回敏感数据；仅输出级评估会严重低估智能体系统的隐私风险。

Conclusion: 智能体管道的每个边界都是潜在的隐私泄露点，需要独立评估；Privacy Flow Graph框架能够有效识别中间信息流中的隐私泄露；当前隐私评估方法严重低估了智能体系统的隐私风险，需要更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly acting on users' behalf, accessing calendars, email, and personal files to complete everyday tasks. Privacy evaluation for these systems has focused on the input and output boundaries, but each task involves several intermediate information flows, from agent queries to tool responses, that are not currently evaluated. We argue that every boundary in an agentic pipeline is a site of potential privacy violation and must be assessed independently. To support this, we introduce the Privacy Flow Graph, a Contextual Integrity-grounded framework that decomposes agentic execution into a sequence of information flows, each annotated with the five CI parameters, and traces violations to their point of origin. We present AgentSCOPE, a benchmark of 62 multi-tool scenarios across eight regulatory domains with ground truth at every pipeline stage. Our evaluation across seven state-of-the-art LLMs show that privacy violations in the pipeline occur in over 80% of scenarios, even when final outputs appear clean (24%), with most violations arising at the tool-response stage where APIs return sensitive data indiscriminately. These results indicate that output-level evaluation alone substantially underestimates the privacy risk of agentic systems.

</details>


### [116] [Modification to Fully Homomorphic Modified Rivest Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04952)
*Sona Alex,Bian Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文档详细介绍了完全同态修改Rivest方案(FHMRS)、FHMRS中的安全问题，以及用于缓解该安全问题的FHMRS修改版本(mFHMRS)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHMRS作为一种完全同态加密方案存在安全漏洞，需要改进以增强其安全性，确保在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 文档首先详细描述FHMRS方案，然后分析其存在的安全问题，最后提出修改方案mFHMRS来缓解这些安全问题。

Result: 提出了mFHMRS方案，该方案在保持FHMRS功能特性的同时，通过修改设计来缓解原始方案中的安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过修改FHMRS方案得到的mFHMRS能够有效缓解原始方案的安全问题，为完全同态加密提供了更安全的实现方案。

Abstract: This document details the Fully Homomorphic Modified Rivest Scheme (FHMRS), a security issue in FHMRS, and a modification to FHMRS (mFHMRS) to mitigate the security issue.

</details>


### [117] [A Practical Post-Quantum Distributed Ledger Protocol for Financial Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05005)
*Yeoh Wei Zhu,Naresh Goud Boddu,Yao Ma,Shaltiel Eloul,Giulio Golinelli,Yash Satsangi,Rob Otter,Kaushik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于格的抗量子加密账本交易方案，解决金融机构隐私与审计需求，引入新型零知识证明技术和紧凑范围证明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融机构存在效率问题，分布式账本技术可解决但面临隐私担忧。现有基于Ring-CT的隐私协议不适合金融机构采用，需要满足保密性和可审计性要求的方案

Method: 提出基于格的抗量子交易方案，利用多种零知识证明技术，引入新的承诺消息等价方法（无需在重新承诺时打开原承诺），构建支持单/多资产的公开可验证交易方案，并设计紧凑范围证明

Result: 构建了满足金融机构保密性和可审计性要求的公开可验证交易方案，支持单资产和多资产场景，提供了安全性分析

Conclusion: 提出的基于格的抗量子加密账本交易方案更适合金融机构采用，所引入的证明技术和构建方法具有独立研究价值

Abstract: Traditional financial institutions face inefficiencies that can be addressed by distributed ledger technology. However, a primary barrier to adoption is the privacy concerns surrounding publicly available transaction data. Existing private protocols for distributed ledger that focus on the Ring-CT model are not suitable for adoption for financial institutions. We propose a post-quantum, lattice-based transaction scheme for encrypted ledgers which better aligns with institutions' requirements for confidentiality and audit-ability. The construction leverages various zero-knowledge proof techniques, and introduces a new method for equating two commitment messages, without the capability to open one of the commitment during the re-commitment. Subsequently, we build a publicly verifiable transaction scheme that is efficient for single or multi-assets, by introducing a new compact range-proof. We then provide a security analysis of it. The techniques used and the proofs constructed could be of independent interest.

</details>


### [118] [Good-Enough LLM Obfuscation (GELO)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05035)
*Anatoly Belikov,Ilya Fedotov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GELO是一种轻量级隐私保护推理协议，通过每批次可逆混合隐藏隐藏状态，防止共享加速器上的KV缓存和隐藏状态被窃取，同时保持低延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在共享加速器上部署时，攻击者可以通过读取设备内存观察KV缓存和隐藏状态，威胁开源模型的提示隐私。现有加密方案（如MPC和FHE）速度太慢，静态混淆方案在多轮统计攻击下会失效。

Method: GELO采用每批次新鲜可逆混合：TEE为每个卸载的投影采样随机矩阵A，计算U=AH，将U和权重W卸载到加速器，返回后应用A⁻¹恢复正确输出。提出两种防御：(i)非正交混合掩蔽Gram矩阵，(ii)正交混合加高能量"屏蔽"向量污染高阶统计。

Result: 在Llama-2 7B上，GELO精确保持float32输出，接近低精度基线，卸载主要矩阵乘法仅增加20-30%延迟开销，成功防御多种ICA/BSS和基于锚点的攻击。

Conclusion: GELO提供了一种实用的隐私保护推理方案，在保持低延迟的同时有效限制不可信加速器观察的信息泄露，解决了现有加密方案速度慢和静态混淆易被破解的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly served on shared accelerators where an adversary with read access to device memory can observe KV caches and hidden states, threatening prompt privacy for open-source models. Cryptographic protections such as MPC and FHE offer strong guarantees but remain one to two orders of magnitude too slow for interactive inference, while static obfuscation schemes break under multi-run statistical attacks once the model is known. We present GELO (Good-Enough LLM Obfuscation), a lightweight protocol for privacy-preserving inference that limits information leakage from untrusted accelerator observations by hiding hidden states with fresh, per-batch invertible mixing. For each offloaded projection, the TEE samples a random matrix A, forms $U = AH$, offloads U and weights W to the accelerator, and then applies $A^-1$ on return, so that $A^-1 ((AH)W ) = HW$ and outputs are unchanged. Because mixing is never reused across batches, the attacker faces only a single-batch blind source separation problem. We analyze information leakage and introduce two practical defenses: (i) non-orthogonal mixing to mask Gram matrices, and (ii) orthogonal mixing augmented with a small fraction of high-energy "shield" vectors that pollute higher-order statistics. On Llama-2 7B, GELO preserves float32 outputs exactly, closely matches low-precision baselines, offloads the dominant matrix multiplications with about 20-30% latency overhead, and defeats a range of ICA/BSS and anchor-based attacks.

</details>


### [119] [Robust Single-message Shuffle Differential Privacy Protocol for Accurate Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05073)
*Xiaoguang Li,Hanyi Wang,Yaowei Huang,Jungang Yang,Qingqing Ye,Haonan Yan,Ke Pan,Zhe Sun,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于洗牌器的自适应分段协议（ASP），用于在纯洗牌模型下进行数值数据分布估计，解决了现有方法在效用、消息复杂度和抗数据投毒攻击鲁棒性三个指标上无法同时优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于洗牌器的差分隐私协议主要关注分类数据的频率估计，但数值数据更为普遍且许多实际应用需要估计具有序数性质的数据分布。现有方法无法在效用、消息复杂度和抗数据投毒攻击鲁棒性三个关键指标上同时取得优异表现。

Method: 提出了ASP协议：1）通过更紧的互信息界进行参数优化设计随机化器；2）设计了具有自适应平滑的期望最大化算法（EMAS）来准确恢复分布并增强鲁棒性；3）提出了新的评估框架来量化不同攻击目标下的鲁棒性。

Result: ASP在三个指标上均优于基线协议：在小ε值时，效用提高了一个数量级，消息复杂度最小，鲁棒性是基线方法的三倍以上。实验表明ASP在所有指标上均表现出色。

Conclusion: ASP协议成功解决了纯洗牌模型下数值数据分布估计的挑战，在保持单消息复杂度的同时，实现了高效用和强鲁棒性，为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Shuffler-based differential privacy (shuffle-DP) is a privacy paradigm providing high utility by involving a shuffler to permute noisy report from users. Existing shuffle-DP protocols mainly focus on the design of shuffler-based categorical frequency oracle (SCFO) for frequency estimation on categorical data. However, numerical data is a more prevalent type and many real-world applications depend on the estimation of data distribution with ordinal nature. In this paper, we study the distribution estimation under pure shuffle model, which is a prevalent shuffle-DP framework without strong security assumptions. We initially attempt to transplant existing SCFOs and the naïve distribution recovery technique to this task, and demonstrate that these baseline protocols cannot simultaneously achieve outstanding performance in three metrics: 1) utility, 2) message complexity; and 3) robustness to data poisoning attacks. Therefore, we further propose a novel single-message \textit{adaptive shuffler-based piecewise} (ASP) protocol with high utility and robustness. In ASP, we first develop a randomizer by parameter optimization using our proposed tighter bound of mutual information. We also design an \textit{Expectation Maximization with Adaptive Smoothing} (EMAS) algorithm to accurately recover distribution with enhanced robustness. To quantify robustness, we propose a new evaluation framework to examine robustness under different attack targets, enabling us to comprehensively understand the protocol resilience under various adversarial scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASP outperforms baseline protocols in all three metrics. Especially under small $ε$ values, ASP achieves an order of magnitude improvement in utility with minimal message complexity, and exhibits over threefold robustness compared to baseline methods.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy for Causal Direction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04473)
*Harikrishnan N B,Shubham Bhilare,Aditi Kathpalia,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于字典模式熵（DPE）的新框架，用于从时间序列符号数据中发现因果方向，通过算法信息论和香农信息论结合，量化模式驱动的组织性，实现可靠因果推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号序列时间观测数据中的因果方向发现具有挑战性，因为缺乏函数模型和噪声假设。需要一种能够识别因果方向及其驱动子模式的通用方法。

Method: 提出字典模式熵（DPE）框架，整合算法信息论和香农信息论。将因果关系解释为候选原因中紧凑、基于规则的模式出现，这些模式系统地约束效应。构建方向特定字典，使用基于熵的度量量化其影响，通过最小不确定性准则推断因果方向。

Result: DPE在多种合成系统中（延迟位翻转扰动、AR(1)耦合、1D斜帐篷映射、稀疏过程）均实现可靠性能，优于或匹配竞争性AIT方法（ETC_E、ETC_P、LZ_P）。在生物和生态数据集中表现有竞争力，而替代方法在特定基因组设置中显示优势。

Conclusion: 最小化模式级别不确定性产生了一个稳健、可解释且广泛适用的因果发现框架。DPE为符号序列因果推断提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Discovering causal direction from temporal observational data is particularly challenging for symbolic sequences, where functional models and noise assumptions are often unavailable. We propose a novel \emph{Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy ($DPE$)} framework that infers both the direction of causation and the specific subpatterns driving changes in the effect variable. The framework integrates \emph{Algorithmic Information Theory} (AIT) and \emph{Shannon Information Theory}. Causation is interpreted as the emergence of compact, rule based patterns in the candidate cause that systematically constrain the effect. $DPE$ constructs direction-specific dictionaries and quantifies their influence using entropy-based measures, enabling a principled link between deterministic pattern structure and stochastic variability. Causal direction is inferred via a minimum-uncertainty criterion, selecting the direction exhibiting stronger and more consistent pattern-driven organization. As summarized in Table 7, $DPE$ consistently achieves reliable performance across diverse synthetic systems, including delayed bit-flip perturbations, AR(1) coupling, 1D skew-tent maps, and sparse processes, outperforming or matching competing AIT-based methods ($ETC_E$, $ETC_P$, $LZ_P$). In biological and ecological datasets, performance is competitive, while alternative methods show advantages in specific genomic settings. Overall, the results demonstrate that minimizing pattern level uncertainty yields a robust, interpretable, and broadly applicable framework for causal discovery.

</details>


### [121] [Bayesian Modeling of Collatz Stopping Times: A Probabilistic Machine Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04479)
*Nicolò Bonacorsi,Matteo Bordoni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文从概率机器学习角度研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)，基于n≤10⁷的数据开发了贝叶斯负二项回归和基于奇块分解的生成模型，发现低阶模结构是τ(n)异质性的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)的统计特性，从概率机器学习视角分析其分布特征，特别是其偏斜、过度离散和算术异质性，旨在建立预测模型并理解其底层机制。

Method: 1. 贝叶斯分层负二项回归(NB2-GLM)：使用log n和n mod 8作为协变量预测τ(n)，通过后验和后验预测分布量化不确定性。2. 基于奇块分解的机制生成模型：将奇数m的3m+1分解为2^{K(m)}m'，随机化块长度，通过Dirichlet-multinomial更新校准。

Result: NB2-GLM在留出数据上获得显著更高的预测似然；将块长度分布条件化到m mod 8可显著改善生成模型的分布拟合，表明低阶模结构是τ(n)异质性的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: Collatz总停止时间τ(n)表现出显著的算术异质性，低阶模结构是其分布特征的关键决定因素；贝叶斯回归模型在预测性能上优于机制生成模型，但后者提供了对底层过程的机制性理解。

Abstract: We study the Collatz total stopping time $τ(n)$ over $n\le 10^7$ from a probabilistic machine learning viewpoint. Empirically, $τ(n)$ is a skewed and heavily overdispersed count with pronounced arithmetic heterogeneity. We develop two complementary models. First, a Bayesian hierarchical Negative Binomial regression (NB2-GLM) predicts $τ(n)$ from simple covariates ($\log n$ and residue class $n \bmod 8$), quantifying uncertainty via posterior and posterior predictive distributions. Second, we propose a mechanistic generative approximation based on the odd-block decomposition: for odd $m$, write $3m+1=2^{K(m)}m'$ with $m'$ odd and $K(m)=v_2(3m+1)\ge 1$; randomizing these block lengths yields a stochastic approximation calibrated via a Dirichlet-multinomial update. On held-out data, the NB2-GLM achieves substantially higher predictive likelihood than the odd-block generators. Conditioning the block-length distribution on $m\bmod 8$ markedly improves the generator's distributional fit, indicating that low-order modular structure is a key driver of heterogeneity in $τ(n)$.

</details>


### [122] [The Volterra signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04525)
*Paul P. Hager,Fabian N. Harang,Luca Pelizzari,Samy Tindel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Volterra签名作为历史依赖系统的显式特征表示，通过张量代数中的时间核加权路径开发，提供学习理论保证，并在动态学习任务中优于经典路径签名基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理非马尔可夫时间序列的方法（如RNN、神经控制微分方程、Transformer）通常依赖隐式记忆机制，这些机制难以解释或在长时域上训练困难。需要一种原则性的显式特征表示来处理历史依赖系统。

Method: 提出Volterra签名VSig(x;K)作为特征表示，通过时间核K对输入路径x进行加权，将其展开到张量代数中。利用Volterra-Chen恒等式建立理论保证，包括可识别性（在增强下的可识别性）和通用逼近定理。对于指数型核，VSig(x;K)在张量代数中求解线性状态空间ODE。

Result: 证明了Volterra签名的可识别性，导出了无限维路径空间上的通用逼近定理。展示了核技巧的适用性，Volterra签名的内积可通过双参数积分方程封闭表征。对于指数型核，VSig(x;K)满足线性ODE。在真实和合成数据的动态学习任务中，该方法持续优于经典路径签名基线。

Conclusion: Volterra签名作为一种稳健、计算可行的特征映射，具有时间重参数化不变性，为历史依赖系统提供了原则性的显式特征表示，在数据科学应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Modern approaches for learning from non-Markovian time series, such as recurrent neural networks, neural controlled differential equations or transformers, typically rely on implicit memory mechanisms that can be difficult to interpret or to train over long horizons. We propose the Volterra signature $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ as a principled, explicit feature representation for history-dependent systems. By developing the input path $x$ weighted by a temporal kernel $K$ into the tensor algebra, we leverage the associated Volterra--Chen identity to derive rigorous learning-theoretic guarantees. Specifically, we prove an injectivity statement (identifiability under augmentation) that leads to a universal approximation theorem on the infinite dimensional path space, which in certain cases is achieved by linear functionals of $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$. Moreover, we demonstrate applicability of the kernel trick by showing that the inner product associated with Volterra signatures admits a closed characterization via a two-parameter integral equation, enabling numerical methods from PDEs for computation. For a large class of exponential-type kernels, $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ solves a linear state-space ODE in the tensor algebra. Combined with inherent invariance to time reparameterization, these results position the Volterra signature as a robust, computationally tractable feature map for data science. We demonstrate its efficacy in dynamic learning tasks on real and synthetic data, where it consistently improves classical path signature baselines.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal Prediction-Augmented Algorithms for Testing Independence of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04635)
*Maryam Aliakbarpour,Alireza Azizi,Ria Stevens*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增强分布测试框架的独立性检验方法，通过利用辅助预测信息在保持最坏情况有效性的同时显著提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非参数有限样本独立性检验存在样本复杂度随支持集大小多项式增长的瓶颈，需要超越最坏情况限制的新方法。

Method: 采用增强分布测试框架，设计包含辅助预测信息的独立性检验器。该方法保持对预测质量无关的鲁棒性，在预测准确时显著提高样本效率。

Result: 开发了：(1)基于预测误差自适应降低样本复杂度的离散分布二元独立性检验器；(2)扩展到高维多变量独立性检验；(3)匹配的最小最大下界证明检验器达到最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过增强分布测试框架成功克服了传统独立性检验的样本效率瓶颈，在保持最坏情况有效性的同时，利用预测信息实现了显著的样本复杂度改进。

Abstract: Independence testing is a fundamental problem in statistical inference: given samples from a joint distribution $p$ over multiple random variables, the goal is to determine whether $p$ is a product distribution or is $ε$-far from all product distributions in total variation distance. In the non-parametric finite-sample regime, this task is notoriously expensive, as the minimax sample complexity scales polynomially with the support size. In this work, we move beyond these worst-case limitations by leveraging the framework of \textit{augmented distribution testing}. We design independence testers that incorporate auxiliary, but potentially untrustworthy, predictive information. Our framework ensures that the tester remains robust, maintaining worst-case validity regardless of the prediction's quality, while significantly improving sample efficiency when the prediction is accurate. Our main contributions include: (i) a bivariate independence tester for discrete distributions that adaptively reduces sample complexity based on the prediction error; (ii) a generalization to the high-dimensional multivariate setting for testing the independence of $d$ random variables; and (iii) matching minimax lower bounds demonstrating that our testers achieve optimal sample complexity.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Optimal Individualized Decision Rules with Conditional Demographic Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05226)
*Wenhai Cui,Wen Su,Donglin Zeng,Xingqiu Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种在个体化决策规则中融入人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束的新框架，通过扰动无约束最优IDR实现高效计算，并在理论和实证上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化决策规则在个性化营销、医疗保健和公共政策设计等社会应用中日益普及，但基于有偏数据训练的IDR可能对性别、种族或语言等敏感属性定义的少数亚群体造成不成比例的伤害，存在歧视性影响的伦理问题。

Method: 提出一个将人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束纳入最优IDR估计的新框架，证明在DP和CDP约束下的理论最优IDR可以通过对无约束最优IDR应用扰动获得，从而实现计算高效的解决方案。

Result: 理论上推导了策略价值和公平约束项的收敛速率，通过综合模拟研究和俄勒冈州健康保险实验的实证应用证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效解决个体化决策规则中的公平性问题，在保持决策效果的同时减少对少数亚群体的歧视性影响，为实际应用提供了理论支持和实证验证。

Abstract: Individualized decision rules (IDRs) have become increasingly prevalent in societal applications such as personalized marketing, healthcare, and public policy design. However, a critical ethical concern arises from the potential discriminatory effects of IDRs trained on biased data. These algorithms may disproportionately harm individuals from minority subgroups defined by sensitive attributes like gender, race, or language. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that incorporates demographic parity (DP) and conditional demographic parity (CDP) constraints into the estimation of optimal IDRs. We show that the theoretically optimal IDRs under DP and CDP constraints can be obtained by applying perturbations to the unconstrained optimal IDRs, enabling a computationally efficient solution. Theoretically, we derive convergence rates for both policy value and the fairness constraint term. The effectiveness of our methods is illustrated through comprehensive simulation studies and an empirical application to the Oregon Health Insurance Experiment.

</details>


### [125] [Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05288)
*Luwei Wang,Nazir Lone,Sohan Seth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类（BSCC）方法，以处理效应为结果指导聚类过程，识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗和政策评估等领域，识别具有相似特征的亚组对于个性化决策至关重要。现有方法多依赖无监督聚类，而监督聚类方法能针对特定结果识别可操作的亚组，但需要进一步发展以处理因果推断问题

Method: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类（BSCC）框架，以处理效应作为指导聚类的结果变量。该方法同时考虑个体的协变量特征和处理效应相似性，识别同质亚组。采用贝叶斯方法进行建模和推断

Result: 在模拟数据集和国际卒中试验第三期（IST-3）的真实世界数据集上评估BSCC，验证了该框架的实用性和有效性

Conclusion: BSCC为识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组提供了一种有效的监督因果聚类方法，在个性化决策中具有重要应用价值

Abstract: Finding patient subgroups with similar characteristics is crucial for personalized decision-making in various disciplines such as healthcare and policy evaluation. While most existing approaches rely on unsupervised clustering methods, there is a growing trend toward using supervised clustering methods that identify operationalizable subgroups in the context of a specific outcome of interest. We propose Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering (BSCC), with treatment effect as outcome to guide the clustering process. BSCC identifies homogenous subgroups of individuals who are similar in their covariate profiles as well as their treatment effects. We evaluate BSCC on simulated datasets as well as real-world dataset from the third International Stroke Trial to assess the practical usefulness of the framework.

</details>


### [126] [How important are the genes to explain the outcome - the asymmetric Shapley value as an honest importance metric for high-dimensional features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05317)
*Mark A. van de Wiel,Jeroen Goedhart,Martin Jullum,Kjersti Aas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出使用非对称Shapley值作为量化混合维度预测模型中特征重要性的更合适替代方法，特别针对临床预测中疾病状态作为基因组效应中介变量的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测中评估高维特征（如基因组学）重要性时，传统方法（通过将其添加到临床变量集后评估预测性能变化）存在问题，因为它未考虑共线性或变量间已知的依赖方向性。

Method: 提出使用非对称Shapley值来量化混合维度预测模型中的特征重要性，特别针对疾病状态作为基因组效应中介变量的临床预测场景，推导了计算局部和全局非对称Shapley值的高效算法。

Result: 局部非对称Shapley值被证明对推断非常有用，而全局非对称Shapley值通过将任何预测性能指标分解为特征的贡献来提供解释。以结直肠癌患者无进展生存期预测为例进行了框架说明。

Conclusion: 非对称Shapley值是量化混合维度预测模型中特征重要性的更合适替代方法，能够解决传统方法在共线性和依赖方向性方面的局限性，特别适用于临床预测场景。

Abstract: In clinical prediction settings the importance of a high-dimensional feature like genomics is often assessed by evaluating the change in predictive performance when adding it to a set of traditional clinical variables. This approach is questionable, because it does not account for collinearity nor known directionality of dependencies between variables. We suggest to use asymmetric Shapley values as a more suitable alternative to quantify feature importance in the context of a mixed-dimensional prediction model. We focus on a setting that is particularly relevant in clinical prediction: disease state as a mediating variable for genomic effects, with additional confounders for which the direction of effects may be unknown. We derive efficient algorithms to compute local and global asymmetric Shapley values for this setting. The former are shown to be very useful for inference, whereas the latter provide interpretation by decomposing any predictive performance metric into contributions of the features. Throughout, we illustrate our framework by a leading example: the prediction of progression-free survival for colorectal cancer patients.

</details>


### [127] [Bayes with No Shame: Admissibility Geometries of Predictive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05335)
*Nicholas G. Polson,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文证明了四种不同的可容许性几何标准（Blackwell风险优势、随时有效可容许性、边际覆盖有效性、Cesàro可接近性）是两两不嵌套的，每种标准都有不同的最优性证明方法，且可容许性是标准相对的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究序列和无分布推断中不同可容许性标准之间的关系，揭示这些几何标准在优化模板上的相似性但在具体实现上的根本差异，证明可容许性判断依赖于具体标准而非绝对概念。

Method: 通过准则分离定理证明四种可容许性类别两两不嵌套；分析每种几何标准的优化模板（贝叶斯风险最小化加可行性约束）但约束集在不同空间、偏序和性能度量上操作；比较鞅相干性在不同标准中的作用。

Result: 四种可容许性标准是几何不相容的：Blackwell可容许性需要鞅相干性但不足以保证；随时有效可容许性在e过程中等价于鞅相干性；覆盖有效性和CAA可容许性不需要鞅相干性；所有标准共享优化模板但约束集不同。

Conclusion: 可容许性是不可约简的标准相对概念，不同几何标准提供不同的最优性证明方法（支撑超平面先验、非负上鞅、可交换性秩、Cesàro引导论证），没有单一的统一框架能包含所有标准。

Abstract: Four distinct admissibility geometries govern sequential and distribution-free inference: Blackwell risk dominance over convex risk sets, anytime-valid admissibility within the nonnegative supermartingale cone, marginal coverage validity over exchangeable prediction sets, and Cesàro approachability (CAA) admissibility, which reaches the risk-set boundary via approachability-style arguments rather than explicit priors. We prove a criterion separation theorem: the four classes of admissible procedures are pairwise non-nested. Each geometry carries a different certificate of optimality: a supporting-hyperplane prior (Blackwell), a nonnegative supermartingale (anytime-valid), an exchangeability rank (coverage), or a Cesàro steering argument (CAA). Martingale coherence is necessary for Blackwell admissibility and necessary and sufficient for anytime-valid admissibility within e-processes, but is not sufficient for Blackwell admissibility and is not necessary for coverage validity or CAA-admissibility. All four criteria share a common optimization template (minimize Bayesian risk subject to a feasibility constraint), but the constraint sets operate over different spaces, partial orders, and performance metrics, making them geometrically incompatible. Admissibility is irreducibly criterion-relative.

</details>


### [128] [On the Statistical Optimality of Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05340)
*Zineng Xu,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为高维回归和分类中的经验风险最小化决策树建立了全面的统计理论，包括尖锐的Oracle不等式、极小极大最优速率，并引入了一种新的函数类来捕捉稀疏性、各向异性平滑度和空间异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全局最优经验风险最小化决策树在计算上已变得可行且在实证上取得成功，但其统计性能的严格理论保证仍然有限。本文旨在为ERM决策树建立全面的统计理论，特别是在高维回归和分类的随机设计背景下。

Method: 使用基于经验局部化Rademacher复杂度的新颖一致集中框架，建立了尖锐的Oracle不等式。引入了一种新的函数类：分段稀疏异质各向异性Besov空间，该空间明确捕捉了实践中遇到的三个关键结构特征：稀疏性、各向异性平滑度和空间异质性。

Result: 建立了ERM估计器相对于最多L个叶子的最佳可能树逼近的过剩风险界限，从而刻画了可解释性-准确性权衡。推导了在PSHAB空间上的极小极大最优速率。主要结果在次高斯性假设下建立，同时也提供了在重尾噪声设置下的鲁棒保证。

Conclusion: 这些发现为ERM决策树的最优性提供了原则性基础，并引入了经验过程工具，这些工具广泛适用于其他高度自适应的数据驱动程序。该工作填补了ERM决策树统计理论的重要空白。

Abstract: While globally optimal empirical risk minimization (ERM) decision trees have become computationally feasible and empirically successful, rigorous theoretical guarantees for their statistical performance remain limited. In this work, we develop a comprehensive statistical theory for ERM trees under random design in both high-dimensional regression and classification. We first establish sharp oracle inequalities that bound the excess risk of the ERM estimator relative to the best possible approximation achievable by any tree with at most $L$ leaves, thereby characterizing the interpretability-accuracy trade-off. We derive these results using a novel uniform concentration framework based on empirically localized Rademacher complexity. Furthermore, we derive minimax optimal rates over a novel function class: the piecewise sparse heterogeneous anisotropic Besov (PSHAB) space. This space explicitly captures three key structural features encountered in practice: sparsity, anisotropic smoothness, and spatial heterogeneity. While our main results are established under sub-Gaussianity, we also provide robust guarantees that hold under heavy-tailed noise settings. Together, these findings provide a principled foundation for the optimality of ERM trees and introduce empirical process tools broadly applicable to other highly adaptive, data-driven procedures.

</details>


### [129] [Harnessing Synthetic Data from Generative AI for Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05396)
*Ahmad Abdel-Azim,Ruoyu Wang,Xihong Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学角度综述了生成式AI模型产生的合成数据的现状，分析了其有效使用的假设条件、优势与局限，并提出了原则性使用框架和实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的兴起极大地扩展了合成数据的可用性和应用范围，但同时也引发了关于合成数据何时能够以有效、可靠和原则性方式使用的根本性统计问题。本文旨在澄清合成数据能够有意义地支持下游发现、推断和预测的假设条件。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法：1) 调查现代生成模型的主要类别及其预期用例；2) 分析合成数据作为真实观测替代品时的常见陷阱；3) 基于这些见解讨论合成数据的原则性使用框架；4) 提出实践建议、开放问题和注意事项。

Result: 论文系统性地识别了合成数据使用中的关键问题：模型误设导致的偏差、不确定性衰减、泛化困难等特征性失效模式。同时总结了合成数据在不同应用场景中的优势和局限性，为原则性使用提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 合成数据在科学、工业和政策领域具有巨大潜力，但其有效使用需要明确的统计假设和原则性框架。论文为方法开发者和应用研究者提供了实用指导，强调了在将合成数据视为真实观测替代品时需要警惕的常见陷阱。

Abstract: The emergence of generative AI models has dramatically expanded the availability and use of synthetic data across scientific, industrial, and policy domains. While these developments open new possibilities for data analysis, they also raise fundamental statistical questions about when synthetic data can be used in a valid, reliable, and principled manner. This paper reviews the current landscape of synthetic data generation and use from a statistical perspective, with the goal of clarifying the assumptions under which synthetic data can meaningfully support downstream discovery, inference, and prediction. We survey major classes of modern generative models, their intended use cases, and the benefits they offer, while also highlighting their limitations and characteristic failure modes. We additionally examine common pitfalls that arise when synthetic data are treated as surrogates for real observations, including biases from model misspecification, attenuated uncertainty, and difficulties in generalization. Building on these insights, we discuss emerging frameworks for the principled use of synthetic data. We conclude with practical recommendations, open problems, and cautions intended to guide both method developers and applied researchers.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Uniform convergence of kernel averages under fixed design with heterogeneous dependent data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04681)
*Danilo Hiroshi Matsuoka,Hudson da Silva Torrent*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文推导了在等间距固定设计点下核平均的均匀收敛速率，适用于强混合和矩条件，无需平稳性假设，并应用于具有时变自回归误差的非参数回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核平均收敛速率研究主要基于随机设计，依赖密度条件论证，而本文针对时间序列分析中常见的等间距固定设计点（如t/T）提供理论框架，弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 利用网格结构分析，在强混合和矩条件下推导弱和强均匀一致性速率，框架适用于依赖三角阵列，特别针对确定性网格上观测的时间序列非参数方法。

Result: 获得了核平均的均匀收敛速率，并将结果应用于具有时变自回归误差的非参数回归模型的局部线性估计量，通过蒙特卡洛实验和实证应用验证理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为等间距固定设计点下的核平均提供了统一的收敛速率理论，补充了现有随机设计结果，为时间序列非参数方法在确定性网格上的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We provide uniform convergence rates for kernel averages on $[0,1]$ under equally-spaced fixed design points of the form $x_{t,T}=t/T,\ t\in\{1,\dotsc, T\},\ T\in\mathbb{N}$. The rates of weak and strong uniform consistency are derived under strong mixing and moment conditions and do not require stationarity. The analysis exploits the grid structure and thus complements existing random-design results such as those of Hansen (2008) and Kristensen (2009), which rely on density-based conditioning arguments. The framework accommodates dependent triangular arrays and is particularly relevant for nonparametric methods applied to time series observed on deterministic grids. As an application, we derive uniform convergence rates for the local linear estimator in a nonparametric regression model with time-varying autoregressive errors. The theoretical results are illustrated through Monte Carlo experiments and an empirical application.

</details>


### [131] [Sequential Multiple Testing: A Second-Order Asymptotic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04685)
*Jingyu Liu,Yanglei Song*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究独立数据流的序贯多重检验问题，建立了二阶渐近最优性的统一理论，证明了几种已知的一阶最优程序实际上也是二阶最优的，并推导了最小可实现期望样本量的二阶渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序贯多重检验方法主要关注一阶最优性（期望样本量与最小可实现期望样本量的比值收敛于1），但缺乏对二阶最优性（两者之差保持有界）的系统研究。需要建立二阶渐近最优性的统一理论框架，并验证现有方法是否满足二阶最优性。

Method: 建立二阶贝叶斯最优性蕴含二阶频率最优性的一般充分条件；证明这些条件对广泛的序贯检验程序类成立；推导最小可实现期望样本量的二阶渐近展开，识别来自多维随机游走边界穿越问题的二阶修正项。

Result: 证明了多种已知的一阶最优程序（包括控制广义族错误率、错误发现率和错误未发现率的方法）实际上是二阶最优的；推导了最小可实现期望样本量的精确二阶渐近展开；建立了贝叶斯与频率最优性之间的二阶等价关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了序贯多重检验中二阶渐近最优性的统一理论，为现有方法提供了更强的理论保证，并提供了更精确的样本量渐近分析框架，对实际应用中的样本量规划有重要意义。

Abstract: We study sequential multiple testing with independent data streams, where the goal is to identify an unknown subset of signals while controlling commonly used error metrics, including generalized familywise rates and false discovery and non-discovery rates. For these problems, procedures that are first-order optimal are known, in the sense that the ratio of their expected sample size (ESS) to the minimal achievable ESS converges to one as the error tolerance levels vanish. In this work, we develop a unified theory of second-order asymptotic optimality. We establish general sufficient conditions under which second-order Bayesian optimality implies second-order frequentist optimality for broad classes of sequential testing procedures. As a consequence, several procedures previously known to be first-order optimal are shown to be second-order optimal: for every signal configuration, the difference between their ESS and the minimal achievable ESS remains uniformly bounded as the error tolerance levels tend to zero. In addition, we derive a second-order asymptotic expansion of the minimal achievable ESS, which refines the classical first-order approximation by identifying the second-order correction term arising from a boundary-crossing problem for a multidimensional random walk. We apply this result to several commonly used error metrics.

</details>


### [132] [The augmented van Trees inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04686)
*Elliot H. Young*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出增强型van Trees不等式，提供比经典van Trees不等式更紧的极小极大贝叶斯风险下界，适用于边界密度非零的先验分布，在非参数估计中可获得更尖锐的常数甚至精确常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典van Trees不等式在估计先验分布边界密度为零时存在局限性，无法获得足够紧的极小极大下界常数。需要一种更灵活的框架来处理更广泛的先验分布，并在非参数估计问题中获得更精确的极小极大风险下界。

Method: 提出增强型van Trees不等式，通过改进经典不等式使其适用于边界密度非零的先验分布。该方法可应用于高斯和非高斯模型、平方损失及其他损失函数，并能扩展到不规则模型的广义van Trees不等式版本。

Result: 1) 在单变量可微且导数Lipschitz的回归函数估计中，获得渐近极小化点态均方误差，常数因子为1.37；2) 在高维Hölder光滑回归函数(光滑度β∈(0,2])估计中获得精确常数；3) 这些结果无法通过经典van Trees不等式获得；4) 方法适用于非高斯模型和非平方损失函数。

Conclusion: 增强型van Trees不等式提供了比经典版本更紧且更灵活的极小极大风险下界框架，能够处理更广泛的先验分布，在非参数估计问题中可获得更尖锐的常数甚至精确常数，为统计推断理论提供了有力的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce an augmented form of the van Trees inequality, that yields uniformly tighter lower bounds on the minimax squared Bayes risk of estimators compared with the classical van Trees inequality. Our augmented inequality also accommodates prior distributions whose densities need not vanish at the boundaries of their supports. We demonstrate how this refinement can be utilised for elementary proofs of a number of minimax lower bounds for nonparametric estimands, that also often attain sharper constants than those obtained by the alternative Le Cam convergence of experiments theory and the classical van Trees inequality, and in some cases obtain exact constants. As an example, our augmented van Trees inequality can be used to obtain the asymptotic minimax pointwise mean squared error when estimating the regression function in the model with normal errors: when the regression function is univariate and differentiable with Lipschitz derivative we obtain this quantity up to a constant factor of $1.37$; and in the high dimensional regime with a Hölder smooth regression function of smoothness $β\in(0,2]$ we obtain exact constants. Both these results do not follow from an application of the classical van Trees inequality. The flexibility of our augmented van Trees inequality accommodates lower bounds for models beyond Gaussianity, loss functions beyond the squared error loss, and we are also able to incorporate this augmentation into generalised versions of the van Trees inequality for irregular models.

</details>


### [133] [Strong consistency of the local linear estimator for a generalized regression function with dependent functional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04690)
*Danilo Hiroshi Matsuoka,Hudson da Silva Torrent*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个广义非参数标量对函数回归模型，用于处理异质分布和强混合数据，建立了局部线性估计量的几乎完全收敛速率，并证明了在依赖数据下收敛速率可能比独立数据更慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于处理实际应用中常见的异质分布和依赖数据场景，特别是在标量对函数回归框架下。传统方法通常假设数据独立同分布，但现实世界数据往往具有依赖性和异质性，需要更一般的理论框架来保证估计量的收敛性质。

Method: 采用局部线性估计方法（FLL）作为标量对函数回归的非参数估计器。研究在广义非参数回归模型框架下，考虑数据具有异质分布和强混合性质。通过理论分析推导了估计量的点态和一致收敛速率，并与局部常数估计（FLC）进行比较。

Result: 1. 建立了局部线性估计量的几乎完全收敛速率；2. 证明了在紧集上点态收敛速率和一致收敛速率相同；3. 发现依赖数据的收敛速率可能比独立数据更慢；4. 模拟研究表明FLL比FLC具有更好的性能；5. 能源消费预测应用中FLL的预测精度显著优于FLC。

Conclusion: 局部线性估计方法在异质分布和强混合数据的标量对函数回归中表现优异，不仅具有理论上的收敛保证，在实际应用中（如能源消费预测）也展现出比局部常数估计更好的预测性能，为处理依赖性和异质性数据提供了有效的非参数回归工具。

Abstract: In this study, we focus on a generalized nonparametric scalar-on-function regression model for heterogeneously distributed and strongly mixing data. We provide almost complete convergence rates for the local linear estimator of the regression function. We show that, under our conditions, the pointwise and uniform convergence rates are the same on a compact set. On the other hand, when the data is dependent, it is proved that the convergence rate can be slower than those obtained for independent data. A simulation study shows the good performance and finite sample properties of the functional local linear estimator (FLL) in comparison to the local constant estimator (FLC). In addition, a one step ahead energy consumption forecasting exercise illustrates that the forecasts of the FLL estimator are significantly more accurate than those of the FLC.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Decorrelating the Future: Joint Frequency Domain Learning for Spatio-temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04418)
*Zepu Wang,Bowen Liao,Jeff,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreST Loss，一种频率增强的时空训练目标，通过联合傅里叶变换在统一的谱域中对齐预测与真实值，以更好地捕捉图结构信号的复杂时空依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准直接预测模型通常依赖点级目标（如均方误差），无法有效捕捉图结构信号中固有的复杂时空依赖。现有频率域方法（如FreDF）虽缓解了时间自相关，但往往忽略了空间和跨时空交互作用。

Method: 提出FreST Loss，通过联合傅里叶变换将监督扩展到联合时空谱域。该方法在统一的谱域中对齐模型预测与真实值，有效解耦空间和时间上的复杂依赖关系。

Result: 理论分析表明该公式减少了时域训练目标相关的估计偏差。在六个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明，FreST Loss具有模型无关性，并能通过更好地捕捉整体时空动态，持续提升最先进基线的性能。

Conclusion: FreST Loss作为一种频率增强的时空训练目标，通过联合谱域监督有效解决了图结构信号预测中复杂时空依赖的建模问题，具有理论优势和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Standard direct forecasting models typically rely on point-wise objectives such as Mean Squared Error, which fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in graph-structured signals. While recent frequency-domain approaches such as FreDF mitigate temporal autocorrelation, they often overlook spatial and cross spatio-temporal interactions. To address this limitation, we propose FreST Loss, a frequency-enhanced spatio-temporal training objective that extends supervision to the joint spatio-temporal spectrum. By leveraging the Joint Fourier Transform (JFT), FreST Loss aligns model predictions with ground truth in a unified spectral domain, effectively decorrelating complex dependencies across both space and time. Theoretical analysis shows that this formulation reduces estimation bias associated with time-domain training objectives. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that FreST Loss is model-agnostic and consistently improves state-of-the-art baselines by better capturing holistic spatio-temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [135] [Delta-Crosscoder: Robust Crosscoder Model Diffing in Narrow Fine-Tuning Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04426)
*Aly Kassem,Thomas Jiralerspong,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delta-Crosscoder改进模型差异分析方法，通过BatchTopK稀疏性和基于变化的损失函数，有效识别微调导致的局部行为变化，在多种模型上优于SAE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Crosscoders方法在处理窄范围微调（行为变化局部且不对称）时存在困难，需要更有效的模型差异分析工具来识别微调引起的内部表示变化。

Method: Delta-Crosscoder结合BatchTopK稀疏性、基于变化的损失函数（优先考虑模型间变化的方向）以及来自匹配输入上配对激活的隐式对比信号，学习基础模型和微调模型之间可解释的潜在方向共享字典。

Result: 在10个模型（包括合成虚假事实、涌现错位、潜意识学习和禁忌词猜测等任务，涵盖Gemma、LLaMA、Qwen等模型，1B-9B参数）上评估，Delta-Crosscoder可靠地分离出对微调行为有因果责任的潜在方向，并实现有效缓解，优于SAE基线，与非SAE方法相当。

Conclusion: Delta-Crosscoder证明Crosscoders仍然是模型差异分析的有力工具，能够有效处理窄范围微调场景，识别微调引起的局部行为变化。

Abstract: Model diffing methods aim to identify how fine-tuning changes a model's internal representations. Crosscoders approach this by learning shared dictionaries of interpretable latent directions between base and fine-tuned models. However, existing formulations struggle with narrow fine-tuning, where behavioral changes are localized and asymmetric. We introduce Delta-Crosscoder, which combines BatchTopK sparsity with a delta-based loss prioritizing directions that change between models, plus an implicit contrastive signal from paired activations on matched inputs. Evaluated across 10 model organisms, including synthetic false facts, emergent misalignment, subliminal learning, and taboo word guessing (Gemma, LLaMA, Qwen; 1B-9B parameters), Delta-Crosscoder reliably isolates latent directions causally responsible for fine-tuned behaviors and enables effective mitigation, outperforming SAE-based baselines, while matching the Non-SAE-based. Our results demonstrate that crosscoders remain a powerful tool for model diffing.

</details>


### [136] [Thin Keys, Full Values: Reducing KV Cache via Low-Dimensional Attention Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04427)
*Hengshuai Yao,Guan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出非对称注意力机制，将查询和键的维度降低到值维度的1/4，通过SVD压缩和QK微调实现75%的键缓存节省，仅带来2%的质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer注意力中查询、键和值使用相同维度，但查询和键仅用于产生标量注意力权重（选择），而值携带丰富的语义表示（值传递）。选择本质上比值传递需要更低的维度，仅需O(log N)维度即可区分N个相关模式。

Method: 提出非对称注意力机制，将查询和键的维度(d_select)降低到值维度(d_model)的1/4。采用SVD压缩现有模型的键参数，然后进行轻量级QK微调（3个epoch，使用少量预训练数据）。通过七个实验验证：位置选择任务、基于内容的检索、WikiText语言建模、GPT-2的SVD压缩、125M参数LLaMA模型验证、Mistral-7B模型应用。

Result: 在WikiText-2和WikiText-103上，d_select = d_model/4仅带来4.3%的困惑度增加，同时减少75%的QK参数。GPT-2的SVD压缩显示键比查询更容易压缩。Mistral-7B模型通过SVD压缩和QK微调实现75%的键缓存节省，仅带来2.0%的质量损失。对于7B参数模型服务128K上下文，非对称注意力为每个用户节省25GB KV缓存，使相同GPU上并发用户数增加约60%。

Conclusion: 注意力机制中的查询、键和值具有不同的功能角色，对称维度设计是不必要的。非对称注意力通过降低查询和键的维度，结合SVD压缩和轻量级微调，可以显著减少KV缓存内存占用，提高模型服务效率，同时保持模型质量。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention uses identical dimensionality for queries, keys, and values ($d_q = d_k = d_v = \dmodel$). Our insight is that these components serve fundamentally different roles, and this symmetry is unnecessary. Queries and keys produce scalar attention weights (\emph{selection}), while values carry rich semantic representations (\emph{value transfer}). We argue that selection is an inherently lower-dimensional operation than value transfer, requiring only $\BigO(\log N)$ dimensions to distinguish among $N$ relevant patterns. We validate this hypothesis across seven experiments: (1)~positional selection tasks requiring just 1~dimension per head, (2)~content-based retrieval requiring $\sim\!\log_2 N$ dimensions, (3--4)~WikiText-2 and WikiText-103 language modeling where $\dselect = \dmodel/4$ incurs only 4.3\% perplexity increase while reducing QK parameters by 75\%, (5)~post-training SVD compression of GPT-2, revealing keys to be far more compressible than queries, with lightweight QK fine-tuning recovering nearly all quality loss, (6)~a 125M-parameter LLaMA model confirming identical degradation ratios across architectures, and (7)~Mistral-7B (7.2B parameters), where SVD compression followed by QK fine-tuning achieves 75\% key cache savings at just 2.0\% residual quality cost. For existing models, SVD compression followed by QK fine-tuning (3 epochs on a small fraction of pretraining data) achieves 75\% key cache savings at $<$2\% residual quality cost. For a 7B-parameter model serving 128K context, asymmetric attention saves 25\,GB of KV cache per user, enabling approximately 60\% more concurrent users on the same GPU.

</details>


### [137] [Agent Memory Below the Prompt: Persistent Q4 KV Cache for Multi-Agent LLM Inference on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04428)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在边缘设备上持久化存储多智能体LLM系统KV缓存的方法，通过4位量化将缓存保存到磁盘，消除重复的预填充计算，显著减少首令牌生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的多智能体LLM系统面临内存管理问题：设备RAM太小，无法同时容纳所有智能体的KV缓存。在Apple M4 Pro上，10.2GB缓存预算只能容纳3个8K上下文的FP16智能体，导致10智能体工作流需要不断驱逐和重新加载缓存，每次驱逐都需要完整的重新预填充（4K上下文下每个智能体15.7秒）。

Method: 系统包含三个组件：1) 块池提供每个智能体隔离的Q4 KV缓存（safetensors格式）；2) BatchQuantizedKVCache支持对多个智能体量化缓存的并发推理；3) 跨阶段上下文注入，在不重新计算的情况下跨对话阶段累积注意力状态。通过将每个智能体的KV缓存以4位量化格式持久化到磁盘，并直接重新加载到注意力层，消除了冗余的O(n)预填充计算。

Result: 在三种架构上评估：Gemma 3 12B、DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite 16B、Llama 3.1 8B。缓存恢复将首令牌生成时间减少高达136倍（Gemma：4K-32K下22-136倍；DeepSeek：4K-32K下11-76倍；Llama：4K-16K下24-111倍；1K下3-10倍）。Q4量化相比FP16在固定设备内存中容纳4倍多的智能体上下文。使用实际Q4 KV缓存测量的困惑度变化：Gemma -0.7%、Llama +2.8%、DeepSeek +3.0%。

Conclusion: 提出的持久化KV缓存方法有效解决了边缘设备上多智能体LLM系统的内存限制问题，通过量化缓存存储和直接恢复机制，显著减少了计算开销，同时保持了可接受的模型质量损失。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems on edge devices face a memory management problem: device RAM is too small to hold every agent's KV cache simultaneously. On Apple M4 Pro with 10.2 GB of cache budget, only 3 agents fit at 8K context in FP16. A 10-agent workflow must constantly evict and reload caches. Without persistence, every eviction forces a full re-prefill through the model -- 15.7 seconds per agent at 4K context. We address this by persisting each agent's KV cache to disk in 4-bit quantized format and reloading it directly into the attention layer, eliminating redundant O(n) prefill computation via direct cache restoration. The system comprises three components: a block pool providing per-agent isolated Q4 KV caches in safetensors format, a BatchQuantizedKVCache for concurrent inference over multiple agents' quantized caches, and cross-phase context injection that accumulates attention state across conversation phases without re-computation. Evaluated on three architectures (Gemma 3 12B, dense GQA, 48 layers; DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite 16B, MoE MLA, 27 layers; Llama 3.1 8B, dense GQA, 32 layers), cache restoration reduces time-to-first-token by up to 136x (Gemma: 22--136x at 4K--32K; DeepSeek: 11--76x at 4K--32K; Llama: 24--111x at 4K--16K; 3--10x at 1K). Q4 quantization fits 4x more agent contexts into fixed device memory than FP16. Perplexity measured with actual Q4 KV caches shows -0.7% for Gemma, +2.8% for Llama, and +3.0% for DeepSeek. Open-source at https://github.com/yshk-mxim/agent-memory

</details>


### [138] [Uncertainty-Calibrated Spatiotemporal Field Diffusion with Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04431)
*Kevin Valencia,Xihaier Luo,Shinjae Yoo,David Keetae Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLID：一种仅从稀疏观测中学习时空动态的掩码条件扩散框架，无需密集场数据或预插值，通过双重掩码目标实现后验采样和校准不确定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场通常只在稀疏、时变的传感器位置观测到，这使得预测和重建成为不适定且需要不确定性量化的问题。现有方法通常在密集再分析或模拟数据上训练，只在测试时处理稀疏性，而SOLID旨在直接从稀疏观测中学习时空动态

Method: SOLID采用掩码条件扩散框架，每个去噪步骤都以测量值及其位置为条件。引入双重掩码目标：(i)强调在未观测空白区域的学习，(ii)对输入和目标提供最可靠锚点的重叠像素进行加权。该框架仅使用稀疏监督进行端到端训练，无需密集场数据或预插值

Result: SOLID在严重稀疏条件下实现了概率误差的数量级改进，产生校准的不确定性地图（ρ > 0.7）。该框架能够生成与测量一致的全场后验采样，在仅使用稀疏观测的情况下显著优于现有方法

Conclusion: SOLID证明了仅从稀疏观测中直接学习时空动态的可行性，通过严格的稀疏条件路径实现了准确的后验采样和校准的不确定性量化，为物理场重建和预测提供了新的端到端解决方案

Abstract: Physical fields are typically observed only at sparse, time-varying sensor locations, making forecasting and reconstruction ill-posed and uncertainty-critical. We present SOLID, a mask-conditioned diffusion framework that learns spatiotemporal dynamics from sparse observations alone: training and evaluation use only observed target locations, requiring no dense fields and no pre-imputation. Unlike prior work that trains on dense reanalysis or simulations and only tests under sparsity, SOLID is trained end-to-end with sparse supervision only. SOLID conditions each denoising step on the measured values and their locations, and introduces a dual-masking objective that (i) emphasizes learning in unobserved void regions while (ii) upweights overlap pixels where inputs and targets provide the most reliable anchors. This strict sparse-conditioning pathway enables posterior sampling of full fields consistent with the measurements, achieving up to an order-of-magnitude improvement in probabilistic error and yielding calibrated uncertainty maps (\r{ho} > 0.7) under severe sparsity.

</details>


### [139] [ZorBA: Zeroth-order Federated Fine-tuning of LLMs with Heterogeneous Block Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04436)
*Chuiyang Meng,Ming Tang,Vincent W. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZorBA：基于零阶优化的联邦微调框架，通过异构块激活机制减少VRAM使用和通信开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型联邦微调面临两大挑战：1）本地更新需要大量VRAM存储梯度；2）频繁模型交换导致显著通信开销。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出ZorBA框架：1）采用零阶优化消除客户端梯度存储需求；2）异构块激活机制，服务器分配不同Transformer块子集给客户端，加速收敛并减少VRAM使用；3）共享随机种子和梯度有限差分减少通信开销；4）理论分析块激活决策对收敛速率和VRAM使用的影响；5）提出ε-约束词典算法优化块激活决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，ZorBA在VRAM使用上比三个联邦微调基线方法最多减少62.41%，同时保持较低的通信开销。

Conclusion: ZorBA通过零阶优化和异构块激活机制，有效解决了大型语言模型联邦微调中的VRAM使用和通信开销问题，为分布式LLM调优提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) enables collaborative tuning across distributed clients. However, due to the large size of LLMs, local updates in federated learning (FL) may incur substantial video random-access memory (VRAM) usage. Moreover, frequent model exchange may lead to significant communication overhead. To tackle these challenges, in this paper we propose ZorBA, a zeroth-order optimization-based federated fine-tuning framework with heterogeneous block activation. ZorBA leverages zeroth-order optimization to eliminate the storage of gradients at the clients by forward passes. ZorBA includes a heterogeneous block activation mechanism in which the central server allocates different subsets of transformer blocks to clients in order to accelerate the convergence rate and reduce the VRAM usage. Furthermore, ZorBA utilizes shared random seeds and the finite differences of gradients in order to reduce the communication overhead. We conduct theoretical analysis to characterize the effect of block activation decisions on the convergence rate and VRAM usage. To jointly enhance the convergence rate and reduce the VRAM usage, we formulate an optimization problem to optimize the block activation decisions. We propose an $ε$-constraint lexicographic algorithm to solve this problem. Experimental results show that ZorBA outperforms three federated fine-tuning baselines in VRAM usage by up to 62.41% and incurs a low communication overhead.

</details>


### [140] [Learning Unified Distance Metric for Heterogeneous Attribute Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04458)
*Yiqun Zhang,Mingjie Zhao,Yizhou Chen,Yang Lu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HARR（异构属性重构与表示学习）范式，用于混合数值和分类属性的聚类分析，通过将异构属性转换为同构状态进行距离度量学习，并与聚类集成实现自适应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实聚类任务中常见包含数值和分类属性的混合数据。数值属性在欧氏空间中有明确定义，而分类属性值代表不同概念且嵌入隐式空间。现有方法要么将异构属性编码为单一类型，要么定义统一度量，未能揭示其内在联系，因此需要研究不同类型属性间的连接

Method: 提出HARR学习范式：1）将异构属性转换为同构状态进行距离度量学习；2）将度量学习与聚类集成，自动适应不同聚类任务；3）将每个属性值投影到统一可学习的多个空间中，更精细地表示和学习分类数据的距离度量。方法无参数、保证收敛，能自适应不同聚类数k

Result: 大量实验表明HARR在准确性和效率方面具有优越性，能够更有效地自适应不同聚类数k

Conclusion: HARR范式通过研究不同类型属性间的连接，将异构属性转换为同构状态进行距离度量学习，并与聚类集成实现自适应，在混合数据聚类中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Datasets composed of numerical and categorical attributes (also called mixed data hereinafter) are common in real clustering tasks. Differing from numerical attributes that indicate tendencies between two concepts (e.g., high and low temperature) with their values in well-defined Euclidean distance space, categorical attribute values are different concepts (e.g., different occupations) embedded in an implicit space. Simultaneously exploiting these two very different types of information is an unavoidable but challenging problem, and most advanced attempts either encode the heterogeneous numerical and categorical attributes into one type, or define a unified metric for them for mixed data clustering, leaving their inherent connection unrevealed. This paper, therefore, studies the connection among any-type of attributes and proposes a novel Heterogeneous Attribute Reconstruction and Representation (HARR) learning paradigm accordingly for cluster analysis. The paradigm transforms heterogeneous attributes into a homogeneous status for distance metric learning, and integrates the learning with clustering to automatically adapt the metric to different clustering tasks. Differing from most existing works that directly adopt defined distance metrics or learn attribute weights to search clusters in a subspace. We propose to project the values of each attribute into unified learnable multiple spaces to more finely represent and learn the distance metric for categorical data. HARR is parameter-free, convergence-guaranteed, and can more effectively self-adapt to different sought number of clusters $k$. Extensive experiments illustrate its superiority in terms of accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [141] [VSPrefill: Vertical-Slash Sparse Attention with Lightweight Indexing for Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04460)
*Chen Guanzhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VSPrefill：一种基于垂直-斜线结构模式的稀疏注意力机制，通过轻量级训练实现长上下文推理加速，在保持98.35%准确率的同时获得4.95倍速度提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力在预填充阶段的二次复杂度阻碍了大语言模型的长上下文推理。现有稀疏注意力方法需要在上下文适应性、采样开销和微调成本之间进行权衡

Method: 提出VSPrefill机制，使用注意力分布中的垂直-斜线结构模式。通过紧凑的VSIndexer模块从增强RoPE的键值表示中预测垂直列和斜线对角线的上下文感知重要性分数，以线性复杂度构建稀疏掩码而不修改主干参数。推理时采用自适应累积阈值策略分配每层稀疏预算，并使用融合内核执行带实时索引合并的注意力

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct和LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上，使用LongBench和RULER基准测试，VSPrefill在128k上下文长度下保持了98.35%的全注意力准确率，同时实现了4.95倍的平均加速

Conclusion: VSPrefill在准确性和效率的权衡中建立了新的帕累托前沿，为长上下文推理提供了一种有效的稀疏注意力解决方案

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of self-attention during the prefill phase impedes long-context inference in large language models. Existing sparse attention methods face a trade-off among context adaptivity, sampling overhead, and fine-tuning costs. We propose VSPrefill, a mechanism requiring lightweight training that uses the vertical-slash structural pattern in attention distributions. Our compact VSIndexer module predicts context-aware importance scores for vertical columns and slash diagonals from key-value representations augmented with RoPE. This approach constructs sparse masks with linear complexity without modifying the backbone parameters. During inference, an adaptive cumulative-threshold strategy allocates sparsity budgets per layer, while a fused kernel executes attention with on-the-fly index merging. Evaluated on Qwen3-4B-Instruct and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct across the LongBench and RULER benchmarks, VSPrefill preserves 98.35% of the full attention accuracy while delivering a 4.95x average speedup at a context length of 128k. These results establish a new Pareto frontier in the trade-off between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [142] [MAD-SmaAt-GNet: A Multimodal Advection-Guided Neural Network for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04461)
*Samuel van Wonderen,Siamak Mehrkanoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAD-SmaAt-GNet是一个用于降水临近预报的多模态物理引导深度学习模型，通过结合多天气变量编码器和物理平流组件，相比基准SmaAt-UNet在4小时预报中将MSE降低了8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值预报方法计算成本高且未能充分利用大量可用天气数据。虽然深度学习模型（特别是CNN）在降水临近预报中显示出潜力，但现有模型如SmaAt-UNet仍可进一步改进，通过整合多模态天气信息和物理约束来提升预报准确性和物理一致性。

Method: 提出MAD-SmaAt-GNet模型，基于SmaAt-UNet架构进行两个关键扩展：(1) 增加额外编码器以学习多个天气变量（多模态输入）；(2) 集成基于物理的平流组件以确保物理一致的预测。模型评估采用四步降水预报，预测时间达4小时。

Result: 每个扩展单独都能改善降雨预报，组合使用时效果更佳。相比基准SmaAt-UNet，MAD-SmaAt-GNet在4小时预报中将均方误差(MSE)降低了8.9%。多模态输入对短期预报特别有益，而平流组件在短期和长期预报范围内都能提升性能。

Conclusion: MAD-SmaAt-GNet成功地将多模态天气信息和物理约束整合到深度学习框架中，显著提升了降水临近预报的准确性。该研究表明结合数据驱动方法和物理知识是改进天气预报的有效途径。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting (short-term forecasting) is still often performed using numerical solvers for physical equations, which are computationally expensive and make limited use of the large volumes of available weather data. Deep learning models have shown strong potential for precipitation nowcasting, offering both accuracy and computational efficiency. Among these models, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are particularly effective for image-to-image prediction tasks. The SmaAt-UNet is a lightweight CNN based architecture that has demonstrated strong performance for precipitation nowcasting. This paper introduces the Multimodal Advection-Guided Small Attention GNet (MAD-SmaAt-GNet), which extends the core SmaAt-UNet by (i) incorporating an additional encoder to learn from multiple weather variables and (ii) integrating a physics-based advection component to ensure physically consistent predictions. We show that each extension individually improves rainfall forecasts and that their combination yields further gains. MAD-SmaAt-GNet reduces the mean squared error (MSE) by 8.9% compared with the baseline SmaAt-UNet for four-step precipitation forecasting up to four hours ahead. Additionally, experiments indicate that multimodal inputs are particularly beneficial for short lead times, while the advection-based component enhances performance across both short and long forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [143] [Understanding the Dynamics of Demonstration Conflict in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04464)
*Difan Jiao,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在上下文学习中容易受到噪声和冲突示例的影响，特别是在规则推断任务中，单个错误示例就能显著降低性能。模型内部采用两阶段计算结构处理冲突证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习使大语言模型能够通过少量示例执行新任务，但这些示例本身可能包含噪声和冲突，使模型能力变得脆弱。研究者希望理解模型如何处理此类冲突，特别是在需要推断潜在模式的规则推断任务中。

Method: 研究模型在规则推断任务中处理冲突证据的行为。使用线性探针和logit lens分析技术，探索模型内部表示。识别不同阶段的关键注意力头：早期到中层的"脆弱头"和晚层的"易受影响头"。通过针对性消融实验验证发现。

Result: 单个错误示例就能导致模型性能显著下降。模型在中间层同时编码正确和错误规则，但在晚层才形成预测置信度。识别出两类关键注意力头：脆弱头对错误示例敏感且存在位置注意力偏差；易受影响头在接触错误证据后显著减少对正确预测的支持。消融少量关键头可将性能提升10%以上。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在上下文学习中处理冲突证据时采用两阶段计算结构，早期编码多种规则可能性，晚期形成最终预测。模型对错误示例的脆弱性源于特定注意力头的敏感性，针对性干预可显著改善性能。这为理解模型推理失败机制和开发更鲁棒的上下文学习方法提供了新见解。

Abstract: In-context learning enables large language models to perform novel tasks through few-shot demonstrations. However, demonstrations per se can naturally contain noise and conflicting examples, making this capability vulnerable. To understand how models process such conflicts, we study demonstration-dependent tasks requiring models to infer underlying patterns, a process we characterize as rule inference. We find that models suffer substantial performance degradation from a single demonstration with corrupted rule. This systematic misleading behavior motivates our investigation of how models process conflicting evidence internally. Using linear probes and logit lens analysis, we discover that under corruption models encode both correct and incorrect rules in intermediate layers but develop prediction confidence only in late layers, revealing a two-phase computational structure. We then identify attention heads for each phase underlying the reasoning failures: Vulnerability Heads in early-to-middle layers exhibit positional attention bias with high sensitivity to corruption, while Susceptible Heads in late layers significantly reduce support for correct predictions when exposed to the corrupted evidence. Targeted ablation validates our findings, with masking a small number of identified heads improving performance by over 10%.

</details>


### [144] [Towards Explainable Deep Learning for Ship Trajectory Prediction in Inland Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04472)
*Tom Legel,Dirk Söffker,Roland Schätzle,Kathrin Donandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于LSTM的船舶轨迹预测模型，通过融入训练得到的船舶领域参数来增强模型可解释性，特别针对内河航道复杂环境，分析了注意力机制中船舶领域参数的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内河航道拥挤环境中船舶轨迹预测对航行安全至关重要。虽然深度学习模型预测精度不断提高，但模型可解释性往往被忽视，这可能掩盖不准确的逻辑并削弱对其可靠性的信心。需要开发既能准确预测又能提供解释的模型。

Method: 采用基于LSTM的船舶轨迹预测模型，融入训练得到的船舶领域参数，通过注意力机制融合交互船舶的隐藏状态。模型变体使用标准位移误差统计进行评估，并分析生成的船舶领域值的合理性。

Result: 在5分钟预测范围内，最终位移误差约为40米，性能与类似研究相当。船舶间注意力架构提高了预测精度，但使用学习到的船舶领域值分配的注意力权重与预期存在偏差。精度提升并非完全由预测轨迹与附近船舶轨迹之间的因果关系驱动。

Conclusion: 该模型通过其内在可解释的设计展示了良好的解释能力。研究发现精度改进不完全由因果关系驱动，这强调了模型可解释性的重要性。未来工作将集中于利用该架构进行反事实分析，并融入更复杂的注意力机制。

Abstract: Accurate predictions of ship trajectories in crowded environments are essential to ensure safety in inland waterways traffic. Recent advances in deep learning promise increased accuracy even for complex scenarios. While the challenge of ship-to-ship awareness is being addressed with growing success, the explainability of these models is often overlooked, potentially obscuring an inaccurate logic and undermining the confidence in their reliability. This study examines an LSTM-based vessel trajectory prediction model by incorporating trained ship domain parameters that provide insight into the attention-based fusion of the interacting vessels' hidden states. This approach has previously been explored in the field of maritime shipping, yet the variety and complexity of encounters in inland waterways allow for a more profound analysis of the model's interpretability. The prediction performance of the proposed model variants are evaluated using standard displacement error statistics. Additionally, the plausibility of the generated ship domain values is analyzed. With an final displacement error of around 40 meters in a 5-minute prediction horizon, the model performs comparably to similar studies. Though the ship-to-ship attention architecture enhances prediction accuracy, the weights assigned to vessels in encounters using the learnt ship domain values deviate from the expectation. The observed accuracy improvements are thus not entirely driven by a causal relationship between a predicted trajectory and the trajectories of nearby ships. This finding underscores the model's explanatory capabilities through its intrinsically interpretable design. Future work will focus on utilizing the architecture for counterfactual analysis and on the incorporation of more sophisticated attention mechanisms.

</details>


### [145] [Augmenting representations with scientific papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04516)
*Nicolò Oreste Pinciroli Vago,Rocco Di Tella,Carolina Cuesta-Lázaro,Michael J. Smith,Cecilia Garraffo,Rafael Martínez-Galarza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对比学习框架，将X射线光谱与科学文献中的领域知识对齐，建立共享的多模态表示，提升物理变量估计精度并识别异常天体目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文学家拥有大量多模态数据（图像、光谱、时间序列）和数十年文献分析，但这些数据源很少被系统整合。需要建立光谱与科学文献之间的连接，以加速对稀有或难以理解天体的解释。

Method: 采用对比学习框架，将X射线光谱与从科学文献提取的领域知识对齐。使用混合专家策略，结合单模态和共享表示。通过光谱检索文本评估对齐效果，并在共享潜在空间中识别异常目标。

Result: 光谱检索文本的Recall@1%达到20%，证明两种模态间存在有意义的对齐。融合光谱和文本数据后，20个物理变量的估计精度比单模态光谱基线提高16-18%。混合专家策略表现最优。在潜在空间中发现异常目标，包括候选脉冲超亮X射线源和引力透镜系统。

Conclusion: 对比学习框架成功建立了光谱与科学文献之间的对齐，加速了天体解释过程。共享潜在空间编码了物理意义信息，混合专家策略效果最佳。该框架可扩展到其他需要对齐观测数据与文献的科学领域。

Abstract: Astronomers have acquired vast repositories of multimodal data, including images, spectra, and time series, complemented by decades of literature that analyzes astrophysical sources. Still, these data sources are rarely systematically integrated. This work introduces a contrastive learning framework designed to align X-ray spectra with domain knowledge extracted from scientific literature, facilitating the development of shared multimodal representations. Establishing this connection is inherently complex, as scientific texts encompass a broader and more diverse physical context than spectra. We propose a contrastive pipeline that achieves a 20% Recall@1% when retrieving texts from spectra, proving that a meaningful alignment between these modalities is not only possible but capable of accelerating the interpretation of rare or poorly understood sources. Furthermore, the resulting shared latent space effectively encodes physically significant information. By fusing spectral and textual data, we improve the estimation of 20 physical variables by 16-18% over unimodal spectral baselines. Our results indicate that a Mixture of Experts (MoE) strategy, which leverages both unimodal and shared representations, yields superior performance. Finally, outlier analysis within the multimodal latent space identifies high-priority targets for follow-up investigation, including a candidate pulsating ULX (PULX) and a gravitational lens system. Importantly, this framework can be extended to other scientific domains where aligning observational data with existing literature is possible.

</details>


### [146] [Invariant Causal Routing for Governing Social Norms in Online Market Economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04534)
*Xiangning Yu,Qirui Mi,Xiao Xue,Haoxuan Li,Yiwei Shi,Xiaowei Liu,Mengyue Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不变因果路由（ICR）框架，用于识别跨异质环境稳定的政策-规范关系，通过反事实推理和不变因果发现分离真实因果效应与伪相关，构建可解释、可审计的政策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线市场经济中，公平曝光、持续参与和平衡再投资等社会规范对长期稳定至关重要。这些规范从无数微观互动中涌现，形成宏观规律，使得因果归因和政策可迁移性变得困难。需要理解驱动这些涌现规范的因果机制，并设计原则性干预措施引导其达到期望结果。

Method: 提出不变因果路由（ICR）框架，整合反事实推理与不变因果发现，分离真实因果效应与伪相关，构建可解释、可审计的政策规则。在基于真实数据校准的异质智能体模拟中进行验证。

Result: 在异质智能体模拟中，ICR相比相关性或覆盖率基线方法，能产生更稳定的规范、更小的泛化差距和更简洁的规则，表明因果不变性为治理提供了原则性和可解释的基础。

Conclusion: 因果不变性为治理提供了原则性和可解释的基础，ICR框架能够识别跨环境稳定的政策-规范关系，有效引导涌现规范向期望方向发展，具有更好的稳定性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Social norms are stable behavioral patterns that emerge endogenously within economic systems through repeated interactions among agents. In online market economies, such norms -- like fair exposure, sustained participation, and balanced reinvestment -- are critical for long-term stability. We aim to understand the causal mechanisms driving these emergent norms and to design principled interventions that can steer them toward desired outcomes. This is challenging because norms arise from countless micro-level interactions that aggregate into macro-level regularities, making causal attribution and policy transferability difficult. To address this, we propose \textbf{Invariant Causal Routing (ICR)}, a causal governance framework that identifies policy-norm relations stable across heterogeneous environments. ICR integrates counterfactual reasoning with invariant causal discovery to separate genuine causal effects from spurious correlations and to construct interpretable, auditable policy rules that remain effective under distribution shift. In heterogeneous agent simulations calibrated with real data, ICR yields more stable norms, smaller generalization gaps, and more concise rules than correlation or coverage baselines, demonstrating that causal invariance offers a principled and interpretable foundation for governance.

</details>


### [147] [An LLM-Guided Query-Aware Inference System for GNN Models on Large Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04545)
*Waleed Afandi,Hussein Abdallah,Ashraf Aboulnaga,Essam Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-WISE：一种面向大知识图谱的任务驱动推理范式，通过分解GNN模型为细粒度组件，结合LLM生成查询模板，实现查询感知的模型实例化，显著提升推理效率和降低内存使用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN推理加速方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏）存在局限性：1）无法根据查询的结构或语义自适应调整模型；2）模型以整体文件存储，需完全加载；3）无法仅加载与目标节点语义相关的邻接节点和模型组件。这导致在大知识图谱上存在大量数据加载冗余和计算冗余。

Method: 1）将训练好的GNN模型分解为细粒度组件，可根据查询子图结构部分加载；2）利用大语言模型（LLM）生成可重用的查询模板，为每个任务提取语义相关的子图；3）实现查询感知的紧凑模型实例化，仅加载与查询语义相关的模型组件。

Result: 在6个大规模知识图谱（最大达4200万节点、1.66亿边）上评估，相比最先进系统：1）推理速度提升最高达28倍；2）内存使用降低98%；3）在商业和开源LLM上均保持或提高了准确性。

Conclusion: KG-WISE通过任务驱动的推理范式，解决了大知识图谱上GNN推理的效率瓶颈，实现了查询感知的模型加载和计算，在保持准确性的同时显著提升了推理性能和内存效率。

Abstract: Efficient inference for graph neural networks (GNNs) on large knowledge graphs (KGs) is essential for many real-world applications. GNN inference queries are computationally expensive and vary in complexity, as each involves a different number of target nodes linked to subgraphs of diverse densities and structures. Existing acceleration methods, such as pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation, instantiate smaller models but do not adapt them to the structure or semantics of individual queries. They also store models as monolithic files that must be fully loaded, and miss the opportunity to retrieve only the neighboring nodes and corresponding model components that are semantically relevant to the target nodes. These limitations lead to excessive data loading and redundant computation on large KGs. This paper presents KG-WISE, a task-driven inference paradigm for large KGs. KG-WISE decomposes trained GNN models into fine-grained components that can be partially loaded based on the structure of the queried subgraph. It employs large language models (LLMs) to generate reusable query templates that extract semantically relevant subgraphs for each task, enabling query-aware and compact model instantiation. We evaluate KG-WISE on six large KGs with up to 42 million nodes and 166 million edges. KG-WISE achieves up to 28x faster inference and 98% lower memory usage than state-of-the-art systems while maintaining or improving accuracy across both commercial and open-weight LLMs.

</details>


### [148] [Oracle-efficient Hybrid Learning with Constrained Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04546)
*Princewill Okoroafor,Robert Kleinberg,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在混合在线学习设置中同时实现统计最优性和计算效率的新算法，通过约束对手从固定函数类中选择标签，并利用截断熵正则化的Frank-Wolfe方法获得与Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合在线学习问题（特征从未知分布中i.i.d.抽取，但标签由对手生成）处于统计学习和完全对抗在线学习之间。先前工作存在二分法：要么统计最优但计算不可行，要么计算高效但统计次优。本文旨在同时实现统计最优性和计算效率。

Method: 考虑结构化设置，约束对手从表达力强但固定的函数类R中选择标签。提出新的学习算法，在给定ERM预言机时高效运行，获得与从学习者假设类H和对手标签类R导出的类的Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。技术工具包括：具有"截断熵正则化"的Frank-Wolfe归约，以及"混合"鞅差序列和的新尾界。

Result: 主要结果是开发了新的学习算法，在给定ERM预言机时高效运行，获得与Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。关键推论是给出了在随机零和博弈中计算均衡的预言机高效算法，适用于动作集高维但支付函数具有低维结构的情况。

Conclusion: 该论文在混合在线学习设置中朝着同时实现统计最优性和计算效率迈出了重要一步，通过结构化约束对手和新的技术工具，为高维随机博弈提供了有效的均衡计算算法。

Abstract: The Hybrid Online Learning Problem, where features are drawn i.i.d. from an unknown distribution but labels are generated adversarially, is a well-motivated setting positioned between statistical and fully-adversarial online learning. Prior work has presented a dichotomy: algorithms that are statistically-optimal, but computationally intractable (Wu et al., 2023), and algorithms that are computationally-efficient (given an ERM oracle), but statistically-suboptimal (Wu et al., 2024).
  This paper takes a significant step towards achieving statistical optimality and computational efficiency simultaneously in the Hybrid Learning setting. To do so, we consider a structured setting, where the Adversary is constrained to pick labels from an expressive, but fixed, class of functions $R$. Our main result is a new learning algorithm, which runs efficiently given an ERM oracle and obtains regret scaling with the Rademacher complexity of a class derived from the Learner's hypothesis class $H$ and the Adversary's label class $R$. As a key corollary, we give an oracle-efficient algorithm for computing equilibria in stochastic zero-sum games when action sets may be high-dimensional but the payoff function exhibits a type of low-dimensional structure. Technically, we develop a number of tools for the design and analysis of our learning algorithm, including a novel Frank-Wolfe reduction with "truncated entropy regularizer" and a new tail bound for sums of "hybrid" martingale difference sequences.

</details>


### [149] [Latent Particle World Models: Self-supervised Object-centric Stochastic Dynamics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04553)
*Tal Daniel,Carl Qi,Dan Haramati,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Aviv Tamar,Deepak Pathak,David Held*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LPWM是一种自监督的物体中心世界模型，能够从视频数据中自主发现关键点、边界框和物体掩码，支持动作、语言和图像目标的条件化，并适用于决策任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个能够从视频数据中自主发现物体表示并适用于决策任务的世界模型，解决现有方法在真实世界多物体数据集上的可扩展性和应用限制。

Method: 提出Latent Particle World Model (LPWM)，通过端到端训练从视频中自主发现关键点、边界框和物体掩码，使用新颖的潜在动作模块建模随机粒子动力学，支持动作、语言和图像目标的条件化。

Result: 在多样化的真实世界和合成数据集上取得了最先进的结果，并成功应用于目标条件模仿学习等决策任务。

Conclusion: LPWM是一个可扩展的物体中心世界模型，能够从视频中自主学习丰富的场景分解，不仅适用于随机视频建模，还能直接应用于决策任务，为自监督学习提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We introduce Latent Particle World Model (LPWM), a self-supervised object-centric world model scaled to real-world multi-object datasets and applicable in decision-making. LPWM autonomously discovers keypoints, bounding boxes, and object masks directly from video data, enabling it to learn rich scene decompositions without supervision. Our architecture is trained end-to-end purely from videos and supports flexible conditioning on actions, language, and image goals. LPWM models stochastic particle dynamics via a novel latent action module and achieves state-of-the-art results on diverse real-world and synthetic datasets. Beyond stochastic video modeling, LPWM is readily applicable to decision-making, including goal-conditioned imitation learning, as we demonstrate in the paper. Code, data, pre-trained models and video rollouts are available: https://taldatech.github.io/lpwm-web

</details>


### [150] [PDE foundation model-accelerated inverse estimation of system parameters in inertial confinement fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04606)
*Mahindra Rautela,Alexander Scheinker,Bradley Love,Diane Oyen,Nathan DeBardeleben,Earl Lawrence,Ayan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了惯性约束聚变中的逆问题，通过微调PDE基础模型来从多模态观测中估计系统参数，在JAG基准测试中取得了优异的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PDE基础模型的下游评估主要关注前向问题（如自回归预测），而逆问题（如从观测反推系统参数）在惯性约束聚变等应用中同样重要。该研究旨在探索基础模型在数据受限的逆问题中的潜力。

Method: 使用开放的JAG基准数据集，该数据集提供每个模拟的高光谱X射线图像和标量观测值。微调预训练的PDE基础模型，并训练轻量级的任务特定头部，以联合重建高光谱图像和回归系统参数。

Result: 微调模型实现了准确的高光谱重建（测试MSE 1.2e-3）和强大的参数估计性能（R^2高达0.995）。数据缩放实验显示，随着训练数据量的增加，重建和回归损失持续改善，在低数据区域获得最大的边际收益。

Conclusion: 从预训练的MORPH权重进行微调优于从头训练相同架构，表明基础模型初始化提高了惯性约束聚变中数据受限逆问题的样本效率，为PDE基础模型在逆问题中的应用提供了实证支持。

Abstract: PDE foundation models are typically pretrained on large, diverse corpora of PDE datasets and can be adapted to new settings with limited task-specific data. However, most downstream evaluations focus on forward problems, such as autoregressive rollout prediction. In this work, we study an inverse problem in inertial confinement fusion (ICF): estimating system parameters (inputs) from multi-modal, snapshot-style observations (outputs). Using the open JAG benchmark, which provides hyperspectral X-ray images and scalar observables per simulation, we finetune the PDE foundation model and train a lightweight task-specific head to jointly reconstruct hyperspectral images and regress system parameters. The fine-tuned model achieves accurate hyperspectral reconstruction (test MSE 1.2e-3) and strong parameter-estimation performance (up to R^2=0.995). Data-scaling experiments (5%-100% of the training set) show consistent improvements in both reconstruction and regression losses as the amount of training data increases, with the largest marginal gains in the low-data regime. Finally, finetuning from pretrained MORPH weights outperforms training the same architecture from scratch, demonstrating that foundation-model initialization improves sample efficiency for data-limited inverse problems in ICF.

</details>


### [151] [Engineering Regression Without Real-Data Training: Domain Adaptation for Tabular Foundation Models Using Multi-Dataset Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04692)
*Lyle Regenwetter,Rosen Yu,Cyril Picard,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREDBench是一个包含83个真实世界表格回归数据集的基准测试，用于研究工程数据与非工程数据的领域差异，并提出基于嵌入引导的合成数据筛选方法，在无需真实工程样本的情况下提升TabPFN 2.5在工程回归任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程应用中的预测建模长期受限于定制化模型和小型孤立表格数据集，限制了大规模学习方法的应用。现有的表格基础模型使用的合成训练分布可能无法反映工程数据的统计结构，限制了向工程回归任务的迁移能力。

Method: 1) 构建TREDBench基准测试，包含83个真实世界表格回归数据集，并标注工程/非工程领域标签；2) 使用TabPFN 2.5的数据集级嵌入研究领域结构；3) 提出嵌入引导的合成数据筛选方法：生成并识别"工程类似"的合成数据集，仅使用选定的合成任务对TabPFN 2.5进行持续预训练。

Result: 工程数据集与非工程数据集部分可区分，而标准程序生成的合成数据集与工程数据集高度可区分，存在显著的合成-真实领域差距。基于合成数据的适应方法在35个工程回归数据集上提升了预测准确性和数据效率：在29/35个数据集上优于TabPFN 2.5，在27/35个数据集上优于AutoGluon，平均数据效率增益分别为1.75倍和4.44倍。

Conclusion: 有原则的合成数据筛选可以将程序生成器转化为领域相关的"数据引擎"，使基础模型能够在真实数据收集是主要瓶颈的数据稀疏科学和工业领域中实现性能提升，而无需依赖真实工程样本。

Abstract: Predictive modeling in engineering applications has long been dominated by bespoke models and small, siloed tabular datasets, limiting the applicability of large-scale learning approaches. Despite recent progress in tabular foundation models, the resulting synthetic training distributions used for pre-training may not reflect the statistical structure of engineering data, limiting transfer to engineering regression. We introduce TREDBench, a curated collection of 83 real-world tabular regression datasets with expert engineering/non-engineering labels, and use TabPFN 2.5's dataset-level embedding to study domain structure in a common representation space. We find that engineering datasets are partially distinguishable from non-engineering datasets, while standard procedurally generated datasets are highly distinguishable from engineering datasets, revealing a substantial synthetic-real domain gap. To bridge this gap without training on real engineering samples, we propose an embedding-guided synthetic data curation method: we generate and identify "engineering-like" synthetic datasets, and perform continued pre-training of TabPFN 2.5 using only the selected synthetic tasks. Across 35 engineering regression datasets, this synthetic-only adaptation improves predictive accuracy and data efficiency, outperforming TabPFN 2.5 on 29/35 datasets and AutoGluon on 27/35, with mean multiplicative data-efficiency gains of 1.75x and 4.44x, respectively. More broadly, our results indicate that principled synthetic data curation can convert procedural generators into domain-relevant "data engines," enabling foundation models to improve in data-sparse scientific and industrial domains where real data collection is the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [152] [Probabilistic Dreaming for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04715)
*Gavin Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于概率方法的Dreamer模型改进，通过并行探索多个潜在状态和保持互斥未来假设，在MPE SimpleTag环境中实现4.5%得分提升和28%回报方差降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dreaming机制使智能体能够从想象经验中学习，实现更鲁棒和样本高效的世界模型学习。现有Dreamer模型在并行探索潜在状态和保持互斥未来假设方面存在局限，需要改进以提升学习效果

Method: 采用概率方法对Dreamer模型进行创新：1) 实现多个潜在状态的并行探索；2) 在保持连续潜在变量梯度特性的同时，为互斥未来维持不同的假设。使用粒子计数K等超参数

Result: 在MPE SimpleTag领域中，该方法优于标准Dreamer模型，实现4.5%的得分提升和28%的回合回报方差降低。展示了改进后的性能优势

Conclusion: 提出的概率方法有效提升了Dreamer模型的性能，但存在超参数（如粒子计数K）随环境复杂度扩展的挑战。未来工作包括优化超参数扩展性和捕捉世界模型中的认知不确定性

Abstract: "Dreaming" enables agents to learn from imagined experiences, enabling more robust and sample-efficient learning of world models.
